# Example multi-array DNA methylation cohort

A
[`methylationCohort()`](https://j-andrews7.github.io/sciVizModules/reference/methylationCohort.md)
of real beta values from the example data in sesameData, on three
Infinium arrays, limited to the CpGs within 5 kb of DNMT1, ADA, MLH1,
BRCA1, TP53, and GNAS. It carries its own probe positions, transcripts,
and cytobands.

## Usage

``` r
data(example_methylation)
```

## Format

A `methylationCohort` on hg38, with 23 samples: 10 normal pancreas
samples from TCGA on HM450, 5 of primary cells (PrEC and NAF) on EPIC,
and 8 of four cell lines (GM12878, K562, LNCaP, and HCT116) on EPICv2.
Its sample table has these columns:

- sample:

  The sample name.

- platform:

  The array.

- cell.type:

  The tissue or cell line.

- category:

  Normal tissue, normal cells, or a cell line.

- global.beta:

  The mean beta value over the CpGs of the whole array.

## Source

Generated from `HM450.10.TCGA.PAAD.normal`, `EPIC.5.SigDF.normal`,
`EPICv2.8.SigDF`, the array manifests, and `genomeInfo.hg38` in the
sesameData package by `data-raw/generate_example_methylation.R`.

## Details

The arrays hold different samples, so differences between them are
biology and platform together. It shows how the module handles mixed
arrays and is not a comparison of them.

## Author

Jared Andrews

## Examples

``` r
library(sciVizModules)
data(example_methylation)
example_methylation
#> methylationCohort: 23 samples on hg38 
#>   HM450         10 samples        432 probes
#>   EPIC           5 samples        533 probes
#>   EPICv2         8 samples        628 probes
#>   405 CpGs shared by HM450 and EPIC
#>   361 CpGs shared by HM450 and EPICv2
#>   451 CpGs shared by EPIC and EPICv2
methylationTrack(example_methylation, gene = "MLH1", group.by = "platform")
```
